                        ><style>
                          .serverSide-gauge { }.serverSide-gauge .ratingMeter { line-height: 1; }.serverSide-gauge .ratingMeter rect { fill: rgb(206, 215, 222); }html[data-theme="dark"] .serverSide-gauge .ratingMeter rect { fill: rgb(44, 73, 97); }.serverSide-gauge .basic rect:nth-child(3) { fill: rgb(194, 48, 36); }.serverSide-gauge .intermediate rect:nth-child(3), .serverSide-gauge .intermediate rect:nth-child(2) { fill: rgb(221, 169, 42); }.serverSide-gauge .advanced rect:nth-child(-n+3) { fill: rgb(35, 149, 99); }.serverSide-gauge .gaugeWrapper { padding: 32px 56px 16px; position: relative; }.serverSide-gauge .gaugeWrapper.compact { padding: 24px 42px 16px; }.serverSide-gauge .gaugeWrapper.noPadding { padding: 32px 0px 16px; }.serverSide-gauge .gaugeWrapper.compact.noPadding { padding: 24px 0px 16px; }.serverSide-gauge .gauge { color: rgb(30, 47, 61); margin: 0px auto; position: relative; }.serverSide-gauge .ratingIndicatorWrapper { border-color: transparent transparent transparent rgb(255, 255, 255); border-style: solid; border-width: 16px 0px 16px 32px; bottom: -16px; height: 0px; position: absolute; z-index: 1; }.compact .serverSide-gauge .ratingIndicatorWrapper { border-width: 12px 0px 12px 24px; bottom: -12px; }.serverSide-gauge .ratingIndicator { border-style: solid; border-width: 12px 0px 12px 24px; height: 0px; left: -30px; position: absolute; top: -12px; width: 0px; }.compact .serverSide-gauge .ratingIndicator { border-width: 8px 0px 8px 16px; height: 0px; left: -22px; position: absolute; top: -8px; }.serverSide-gauge .ratingIndicator.basic { border-color: transparent transparent transparent rgb(194, 48, 36); }.serverSide-gauge .ratingIndicator.intermediate { border-color: transparent transparent transparent rgb(221, 169, 42); }.serverSide-gauge .ratingIndicator.advanced { border-color: transparent transparent transparent rgb(35, 149, 99); }.serverSide-gauge .ratingTextWrapper { bottom: 0px; left: 0px; position: absolute; text-align: center; width: 100%; }.serverSide-gauge .ratingLabel { font-size: 12px; }.serverSide-gauge .achievableLimitLabel { bottom: 0px; color: rgb(30, 47, 61); font-size: 18px; font-weight: 700; line-height: 24px; position: absolute; width: 36px; z-index: 1; }.compact .serverSide-gauge .achievableLimitLabel { font-size: 14px; width: 30px; }.serverSide-gauge .achievableLimitLabel::after { background-color: rgb(30, 47, 61); content: ""; height: 1px; position: absolute; top: calc(50% - 1px); width: 14px; }.compact .serverSide-gauge .achievableLimitLabel::after { width: 8px; }.serverSide-gauge .achievableLimitLabelLow { bottom: 21px; left: -7px; }.serverSide-gauge .achievableLimitLabelLow::after { right: -18px; }.compact .serverSide-gauge .achievableLimitLabelLow::after { right: -15px; }.serverSide-gauge .achievableLimitLabelHigh { bottom: 26px; right: -7px; }.serverSide-gauge .achievableLimitLabelHigh::after { left: -18px; }.compact .serverSide-gauge .achievableLimitLabelHigh::after { left: -15px; }.serverSide-gauge .limitsHover { display: flex; height: 0px; justify-content: space-between; margin: 0px auto; position: relative; }.serverSide-gauge .limitHover { height: 30px; }.serverSide-gauge .limits { color: rgb(30, 47, 61); font-size: 12px; left: 50%; position: absolute; transform: translateX(-50%); }.serverSide-gauge .limitLabel { text-align: center; }.serverSide-gauge .gaugeSvgPattern { stroke: rgb(206, 215, 222); stroke-width: 3; }.serverSide-gauge .pill { align-items: center; border-radius: 2px; display: inline-flex; font-weight: bold; gap: 4px; justify-content: center; max-width: 100%; text-align: center; white-space: nowrap; }.serverSide-gauge .pill.showClose, .serverSide-gauge .pill.showRevert { padding-right: 0px; }.serverSide-gauge .pill.labelHidden { border-radius: 40px; }html[data-theme="dark"] .serverSide-gauge .pill { box-shadow: rgb(37, 60, 79) 0px 0px 0px 1px; }.serverSide-gauge .pill + .pill { margin-left: 8px; }.serverSide-gauge .pill--large { font-size: 14px; height: 40px; line-height: 40px; padding: 0px 12px; }.serverSide-gauge .pill--regular { font-size: 13px; height: 24px; line-height: 24px; padding: 0px 8px; }.serverSide-gauge .pill--compressed { font-size: 12px; height: 20px; line-height: 20px; padding: 0px 4px; }.serverSide-gauge .pill--tiny { font-size: 10px; height: 16px; line-height: 16px; padding: 0px 4px; }.serverSide-gauge .pill--primary { background: rgb(0, 117, 192); color: rgb(255, 255, 255); }.serverSide-gauge .pill--primary-outline { background: rgb(255, 255, 255); box-shadow: rgb(0, 117, 192) 0px 0px 0px 1px inset; color: rgb(0, 117, 192); }.serverSide-gauge .pill--standard { background: rgb(206, 215, 222); color: rgb(30, 47, 61); }.serverSide-gauge .pill--standard-outline { background: rgb(255, 255, 255); box-shadow: rgb(133, 153, 170) 0px 0px 0px 1px inset; color: rgb(30, 47, 61); }.serverSide-gauge .pill--tag { background: rgb(255, 255, 255); box-shadow: rgb(1, 70, 114) 0px 0px 0px 1px inset; color: rgb(1, 70, 114); font-weight: bold; }.serverSide-gauge .pill--tag-public { background: rgb(147, 195, 227); color: rgb(23, 35, 44); font-weight: bold; }.serverSide-gauge .pill--private { background: rgb(147, 195, 227); color: rgb(23, 35, 44); font-weight: bold; }.serverSide-gauge .pill--public-inherited { background: rgb(243, 245, 247); box-shadow: rgb(133, 153, 170) 0px 0px 0px 1px inset; color: rgb(23, 35, 44); font-weight: bold; }.serverSide-gauge .pill--private-inherited { background: rgb(243, 245, 247); box-shadow: rgb(133, 153, 170) 0px 0px 0px 1px inset; color: rgb(23, 35, 44); font-weight: bold; }.serverSide-gauge .pill--deleted { background: rgb(255, 204, 198); color: rgb(30, 47, 61); font-weight: bold; }.serverSide-gauge .pill--revert-delete { background: rgb(255, 204, 198); color: rgb(30, 47, 61); font-weight: bold; }.serverSide-gauge .disabled { opacity: 0.7; pointer-events: none; }.serverSide-gauge .content { overflow-x: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }.serverSide-gauge .pillIcon { flex-shrink: 0; }.serverSide-gauge .close { align-items: center; appearance: none; background: none; border: 0px none; border-bottom-right-radius: 2px; border-top-right-radius: 2px; cursor: pointer; display: flex; flex-shrink: 0; height: 100%; justify-content: center; padding: 0px; }.serverSide-gauge .close:hover, .serverSide-gauge .close.\:hover, .serverSide-gauge .close:active { background: rgba(15, 22, 28, 0.05); }.serverSide-gauge .close--large { width: 40px; }.serverSide-gauge .close--regular { width: 24px; }.serverSide-gauge .close--compressed { width: 20px; }.serverSide-gauge .close--tiny { width: 16px; }.serverSide-gauge .close--primary { color: rgb(255, 255, 255); }.serverSide-gauge .close--primary:hover, .serverSide-gauge .close--primary.\:hover { color: rgb(255, 255, 255); }.serverSide-gauge .close--primary-outline { color: rgb(37, 60, 79); }.serverSide-gauge .close--primary-outline:hover, .serverSide-gauge .close--primary-outline.\:hover { color: rgb(15, 22, 28); }.serverSide-gauge .close--standard { color: rgb(37, 60, 79); }.serverSide-gauge .close--standard:hover, .serverSide-gauge .close--standard.\:hover { color: rgb(15, 22, 28); }.serverSide-gauge .close--standard-outline { color: rgb(37, 60, 79); }.serverSide-gauge .close--standard-outline:hover, .serverSide-gauge .close--standard-outline.\:hover { color: rgb(15, 22, 28); }.serverSide-gauge .bs-icon { display: inline-block; vertical-align: text-bottom; }.serverSide-gauge .bs-icon .brandFill { fill: rgb(249, 73, 23); }.serverSide-gauge .bs-icon .lightSlateFill { fill: rgb(44, 73, 97); }.serverSide-gauge .bs-icon .ratingFillAdvanced { fill: rgb(35, 149, 99); }.serverSide-gauge .bs-icon .ratingFillIntermediate { fill: rgb(221, 169, 42); }.serverSide-gauge .bs-icon .ratingFillBasic { fill: rgb(194, 48, 36); }.serverSide-gauge .bs-icon &gt; * { display: block; }.bucket { font-weight: 700; }
                        </style>
